phi1, eta2, phi2):\n    return np.hypot(eta1 - eta2, delta_phi(phi1, phi2))\n\n\ndef roc_auc(y_true, score):\n    \"\"\"Rank-based AUC (handles ties), no sklearn available.\"\"\"\n    y = np.asarray(y_true).astype(np.int8)\n    s = np.asarray(score, dtype=np.float64)\n    n_pos = int((y == 1).sum())\n    n_neg = int((y == 0).sum())\n    if n_pos == 0 or n_neg == 0:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    order = np.argsort(s, kind=\"mergesort\")\n    s_sorted = s[order]\n    ranks = np.empty(len(s), dtype=np.float64)\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(s):\n        j = i\n        while j + 1 < len(s) and s_sorted[j + 1] == s_sorted[i]:\n            j += 1\n        ranks[order[i : j + 1]] = 0.5 * (i + j) + 1.0\n        i = j + 1\n    return float((ranks[y == 1].sum() - n_pos * (n_pos + 1) / 2.0) / (n_pos * n_neg))\n\n\ndef average_precision(y_true, score):\n    y = np.asarray(y_true).astype(np.int8)\n    s = np.asarray(score, dtype=np.float64)\n    n_pos = int((y == 1).sum())\n    if n_pos == 0:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    order = np.argsort(-s, kind=\"mergesort\")\n    y_s = y[order]\n    tp = np.cumsum(y_s)\n    precision = tp / np.arange(1, len(y_s) + 1)\n    return float((precision * y_s).sum() / n_pos)\n\n\ndef binary_metrics(y_true, score, threshold=0.5):\n    y = np.asarray(y_true).astype(np.int8)\n    p = (np.asarray(score) >= threshold).astype(np.int8)\n    tp = int(((y == 1) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fp = int(((y == 0) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fn = int(((y == 1) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    tn = int(((y == 0) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    prec = tp / (tp + fp) if tp + fp else 0.0\n    rec = tp / (tp + fn) if tp + fn else 0.0\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if prec + rec else 0.0\n    s = np.clip(np.asarray(score, dtype=np.float64), 1e-9, 1 - 1e-9)\n    logloss = float(-np.mean(y * np.log(s) + (1 - y) * np.log(1 - s)))\n    return dict(\n        threshold=float(threshold), tp=tp, fp=fp, fn=fn, tn=tn,\n        accuracy=(tp + tn) / max(len(y), 1), dtype=np.int32)\n        rows.append(pd.DataFrame(rec))\n\n    df = pd.concat(rows, ignore_index=True)\n    df.attrs[\"n_truth_total\"] = n_truth_total\n    df.attrs[\"n_truth_reachable\"] = n_truth_reachable\n    return df\n\n\ndef _triplet_features(pt, eta, phi, m, bt, px, py, pz, e,\n                      ia, ib, ic, ht, n_all, n_btag_ev):\n    d = {}\n    pt_a, pt_b, pt_c = pt[ia], four-vectors -------------------------------\n    def pair(u, v):\n        PX, PY, PZ, E = px[u] + px[v], py[u] + py[v], pz[u] + pz[v], e[u] + e[v]\n        return _mass(PX, PY, PZ, E), np.hypot(PX, PY)\n\n    m_ab, ptp_ab = pair(ia, ib)\n    m_ac, ptp_ac = pair(ia, ic)\n    m_bc, ptp_bc = pair(ib, ic)\n\n    PX = px[ia] + px[ib] + px[ic]\n    PY = py[ia] + py[ib] + py[ic]\n    PZ = pz[ia] + pz[ib] + pz[ic]\n    E = e[ia] + e[ib] + e[ic]\n    m123 = _mass(PX, PY, PZ, E)\n    pt123 = np.hypot(PX, PY)\n    p123 = np.sqrt(PX * PX + PY * PY + PZ * PZ)\n    eta123 = np.arcsinh(np.divide(PZ, np.maximum(pt123, 1e-9)))\n    phi123 = np.arctan2(PY, PX)\n    denom = np.maximum(m123, 1e-9)\n\n    dr_ab = delta_r(eta_a, phi_a, eta_b, phi_b)\n    dr_ac = delta_r(eta_a, phi_a, eta_c, phi_c)\n    dr_bc = delta_r(eta_b, phi_b, eta_c, phi_c)\n\n    # ---- required schema columns ---------------------------------------\n    d[\"dr_ab\"], d[\"dr_ac\"], d[\"dr_bc\"] = dr_ab, dr_ac, dr_bc\n    d[\"mij_over_m123_ab\"] = m_ab / denom\n    d[\"mij_over_m123_ac\"] = m_ac / denom\n    d[\"mij_over_m123_bc\"] = m_bc / denom\n\n    # ---- single-jet kinematics ------------------------------------------\n    d[\"pt_a\"], d[\"pt_b\"], d[\"pt_c\"] = pt_a, pt_b, pt_c\n    d[\"abseta_a\"], d[\"abseta_b\"], d[\"abseta_c\"] = np.abs(eta_a), np.abs(eta_b), np.abs(eta_c)\n    d[\"m_a\"], d[\"m_b\"], d[\"m_c\"] = m[ia], m[ib], m[ic]\n    d[\"btag_a\"], d[\"btag_b\"], d[\"btag_c\"] = bt_a, bt_b, bt_c\n    n_btag = bt_a + bt_b + bt_c\n    d[\"n_btag\"] = pair masses -----------------------------------------------------\n    d[\"m_ab\"], d[\"m_ac\"], d[\"m_bc\"] = m_ab, m_ac, m_bc\n    pair_m = np.stack([m_ab, m_ac, m_bc], axis=1)\n    pair_dr = np.stack([dr_ab, dr_ac, dr_bc], axis=1)\n    pair_pt = np.stack([ptp_ab, ptp_ac, ptp_bc], axis=1)\n    d[\"m_pair_min\"] = pair_m.min(axis=1)\n    d[\"m_pair_max\"] = pair_m.max(axis=1)\n    d[\"m_pair_med\"] = np.median(pair_m, axis=1)\n\n    # W candidate = pair with mass closest to m_W\n    w_idx = np.argmin(np.abs(pair_m - M_W), axis=1)\n    rowsel = np.arange(len(ia))\n    m_w = pair_m[rowsel, w_idx]\n    d[\"m_wcand\"] = m_w\n    d[\"dm_wcand\"] = m_w - M_W\n    d[\"absdm_wcand\"] = np.abs(m_w - M_W)\n    d[\"dr_wcand\"] = pair_dr[rowsel, w_idx]\n    d[\"pt_wcand\"] = pair_pt[rowsel, w_idx]\n    d[\"mw_over_m123\"] = m_w / denom\n\n    # b-tag driven W candidate: pair of the two non-b jets when exactly 1 b-tag\n    btmat = np.stack([bt_a, bt_b, bt_c], axis=1)\n    # index of the (first) b-tagged jet, -1 if none\n    has_b = btmat.max(axis=1) > 0\n    b_pos = np.where(has_b, np.argmax(btmat, axis=1), -1)\n    # pair opposite jet p: 0->bc, 1->ac, 2->ab\n    opp = np.stack([m_bc, m_ac, m_ab], axis=1)\n    opp_dr = np.stack([dr_bc, dr_ac, dr_ab], axis=1)\n    m_wb = np.where(has_b, opp[rowsel, np.maximum(b_pos, 0)], m_w)\n    d[\"m_w_nonb\"] = m_wb\n    d[\"absdm_w_nonb\"] = np.abs(m_wb - M_W)\n    d[\"dr_w_nonb\"] = np.where(has_b, opp_dr[rowsel, np.maximum(b_pos, 0)], d[\"dr_wcand\"])\n    pt_arr = np.stack([pt_a, pt_b, pt_c], axis=1)\n    d[\"pt_bjet\"] = np.where(has_b, pt_arr[rowsel, np.maximum(b_pos, 0)], 0.0)\n\n    # ---- triplet-level ---------------------------------------------------\n    d[\"m123\"] = m123\n    d[\"pt123\"] = pt123\n    d[\"abseta123\"] = np.abs(eta123)\n    d[\"absdm_top\"] = np.abs(m123 - M_TOP)\n    d[\"m123_over_ht\"] = m123 / max(ht, 1e-9)\n    d[\"pt123_over_ht\"] = pt123 / max(ht, 1e-9)\n    sum_pt = pt_a + pt_b + pt_c\n    d[\"sumpt_over_ht\"] = sum_pt / max(ht, 1e-9)\n    d[\"sumpt\"] = sum_pt\n    d[\"pt_frac_a\"] = pt_a / sum_pt\n    d[\"pt_frac_b\"] = pt_b / sum_pt\n    d[\"pt_frac_c\"] = pt_c / sum_pt\n    d[\"pt123_over_m123\"] = pt123 / denom\n    d[\"boost\"] = p123 / denom\n\n    d[\"dr_sum\"] = dr_ab + dr_ac + dr_bc\n    d[\"dr_min\"] = np.minimum(np.minimum(dr_ab, dr_ac), dr_bc)\n    d[\"dr_max\"] = np.maximum(np.maximum(dr_ab, dr_ac), dr_bc)\n    d[\"deta_max\"] = np.maximum.reduce(\n        [np.abs(eta_a - eta_b), np.abs(eta_a - eta_c), np.abs(eta_b - eta_c)]\n    )\n    # dR of each jet to the triplet axis\n    d[\"dr_a_123\"] = delta_r(eta_a, phi_a, eta123, phi123)\n    d[\"dr_b_123\"] = delta_r(eta_b, phi_b, eta123, phi123)\n    d[\"dr_c_123\"] = delta_r(eta_c, phi_c, eta123, phi123)\n\n    # ---- event context ---------------------------------------------------\n    d[\"n_genjet\"] = np.full(len(ia), float(n_all))\n    d[\"ht_event\"] = np.full(len(ia), ht)\n    d[\"idx_i\"] = ia.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_j\"] = ib.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_k\"] = ic.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_sum\"] = (ia + ib + ic).astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_max_frac\"] = ic.astype(np.float64) / max(float(n_all) - 1.0, 1.0)\n    return d\n\n\nFEATURE_EXCLUDE = {\"event_id\", \"i\", \"j\", \"k\", \"is_truth\", --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# stage 5: selection\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef greedy_disjoint_select(df: pd.DataFrame, score_col: str = \"score_xgb\",\n                           max_per_event: int = 2, threshold: float = -np.inf,\n                           second_threshold: float | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Per event: take the highest-scoring triplet, then the highest-scoring\n    remaining triplet sharing no jet index with anything already taken.\"\"\"\n np.asarray(y_true).astype(np.int8)\n    s = np.asarray(score, dtype=np.float64)\n    n_pos = int((y == 1).sum())\n    n_neg = int((y == 0).sum())\n    if n_pos == 0 or n_neg == 0:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    order = np.argsort(s, kind=\"mergesort\")\n    s_sorted = s[order]\n    ranks = np.empty(len(s), dtype=np.float64)\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(s):\n        j = i\n        while j + 1 < len(s) and s_sorted[j + 1] == s_sorted[i]:\n            j += 1\n        ranks[order[i : j + 1]] = 0.5 * (i + j) + 1.0\n        i = j + 1\n    return float((ranks[y == 1].sum() - n_pos * (n_pos + if n_pos == 0:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    order = np.argsort(-s, kind=\"mergesort\")\n    y_s = y[order]\n    tp = np.cumsum(y_s)\n    precision = tp / np.arange(1, len(y_s) + 1)\n    return tp = int(((y == 1) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fp = int(((y == 0) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fn = int(((y == 1) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    tn = int(((y == 0) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    prec = tp / (tp + fp) if tp tp = int(((y == 1) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fp = int(((y == 0) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fn = int(((y == 1) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    tn = int(((y == 0) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    prec = tp / (tp + fp) if tp d[\"m_pair_min\"] = pair_m.min(axis=1)\n    d[\"m_pair_max\"] = pair_m.max(axis=1)\n    d[\"m_pair_med\"] = np.median(pair_m, axis=1)\n\n    # W candidate = pair with mass closest to m_W\n    w_idx = 1)\n\n\ndef _event_context_features(pt, eta, phi, m, bt, px, py, pz, e, combos,\n                            base, n, ht):\n    \"\"\"Features that compare a triplet with the rest of its own greedy_disjoint_select(df: pd.DataFrame, score_col: str = \"score_xgb\",\n                           max_per_event: int = 2, threshold: float = -np.inf,\n                           second_threshold: float | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Per event: take the highest-scoring triplet, then the highest-scoring\n    remaining triplet sharing no jet index with anything already taken.\"\"\"\n tp = int(((y == 1) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fp = int(((y == 0) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fn = int(((y == 1) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    tn = int(((y == 0) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    prec = tp / (tp + fp) if tp + fp else 0.0\n    rec = tp / (tp + fn) if tp + fn else 0.0\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if prec + rec else 0.0\n    s = np.clip(np.asarray(score, dtype=np.float64), 1e-9, 1 - 1e-9)\n    logloss = float(-np.mean(y * np.log(s) + (1 - y) * np.log(1 - s)))\n    return dict(\n        threshold=float(threshold), tp=tp, fp=fp, fn=fn, tn=tn,\n        accuracy=(tp + tn) / max(len(y), 1), --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nJET_BRANCHES = [\n    \"Number\", \"N_genjet\",\n    \"genjet_pt\", \"genjet_eta\", \"genjet_phi\", \"genjet_m\", greedy_disjoint_select(df: pd.DataFrame, score_col: str = \"score_xgb\",\n                           max_per_event: int = 2, threshold: float = -np.inf,\n                           second_threshold: float | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Per event: take the highest-scoring triplet, then the highest-scoring\n    remaining triplet sharing no jet index with anything already taken.\"\"\"\n          max_per_event: int = 2, threshold: float = -np.inf,\n                           second_threshold: float | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Per event, pick the jet-disjoint set of <= `max_per_event` triplets that\n    maximises the summed classifier probability.\n\n    Because the target metric is pure triplet-level recall, the expected number\n    of recovered truth triplets for a chosen set S is sum_{t in S} P(t truthy).\n    Maximising the summed calibrated probability under the disjointness and\n    multiplicity constraints is therefore the Bayes-optimal decision rule --\n    strictly better than the greedy argmax-then-best-disjoint heuristic, which\n    can be forced into a bad second pick by a slightly-higher-scoring fake.\n    \"\"\"\n    if second_threshold is s[best1]))\n            continue\n        bits = np.zeros(len(g), dtype=np.int64)\n        for c in range(3):\n            bits |= (np.int64(1) << ijk[:, c])\n        comp = (bits[:, None] & bits[None, :]) == 0\n        np.fill_diagonal(comp, False)\n        tot = np.where(comp, s[:, None] + s[None, :], -np.inf)\n        pair_best = tot.max()\n        if np.isfinite(pair_best):\n denom\n\n    d[\"dr_sum\"] = dr_ab + dr_ac + dr_bc\n    d[\"dr_min\"] = np.minimum(np.minimum(dr_ab, dr_ac), dr_bc)\n    d[\"dr_max\"] = np.maximum(np.maximum(dr_ab, dr_ac), dr_bc)\n    d[\"deta_max\"] = np.maximum.reduce(\n        [np.abs(eta_a - eta_b), np.abs(eta_a - eta_c), np.abs(eta_b - eta_c)]\n    )\n    # dR of each jet to the triplet axis\n    d[\"dr_a_123\"] = delta_r(eta_a, phi_a, eta123, phi123)\n    d[\"dr_b_123\"] = delta_r(eta_b, phi_b, eta123, phi123)\n    d[\"dr_c_123\"] = delta_r(eta_c, phi_c, eta123, phi123)\n\n    # ---- W-mass-constrained top mass -------------------------------------\n    # Rescaling the triplet mass by M_W / m(non-b pair) cancels most of the\n    # jet-energy-scale / out-of-cone smearing and is a far sharper top\n    # discriminant than the raw m123.\n    m123_wcorr = m123 * M_W / np.maximum(m_wb, 1e-6)\n    d[\"m123_wcorr\"] = m123_wcorr\n    d[\"absdm_top_wcorr\"] = np.abs(m123_wcorr - M_TOP)\n    d[\"m123_minus_mw\"] = m123 - m_wb\n    chi2 = ((m123 - M_TOP) / 20.0) ** 2 + ((m_wb - M_W) / 12.0) ** 2\n    d[\"chi2_topw\"] = chi2\n    d[\"log_chi2_topw\"] = np.log1p(chi2)\n\n    # ---- decay angles ----------------------------------------------------\n    # cos(theta*) of the b jet in the triplet rest frame w.r.t. the triplet\n    # flight direction: flat for a real top decay, peaked for random combos.\n    bx, by, bz = PX / np.maximum(E, 1e-9), PY / np.maximum(E, 1e-9), PZ / np.maximum(E, 1e-9)\n    b2 = bx * bx + by * by + bz * bz\n    gamma = 1.0 / np.sqrt(np.maximum(1.0 - b2, 1e-12))\n    px3 = np.stack([px[ia], px[ib], px[ic]], axis=1)\n    py3 = np.stack([py[ia], py[ib], py[ic]], axis=1)\n    pz3 = np.stack([pz[ia], pz[ib], pz[ic]], axis=1)\n    e3 = np.stack([e[ia], e[ib], e[ic]], axis=1)\n    sel_b = np.maximum(b_pos, 0)\n    jpx, jpy, jpz, je = (px3[rowsel, sel_b], py3[rowsel, sel_b],\n                         pz3[rowsel, sel_b], e3[rowsel, sel_b])\n    bp = bx * jpx + by * jpy + bz * jpz\n    fac = (gamma - 1.0) * np.divide(bp, np.maximum(b2, 1e-12)) - gamma * je\n    rpx, rpy, rpz = jpx + fac * bx, jpy + fac * by, jpz + fac * bz\n    rmag = np.sqrt(rpx * rpx + rpy * rpy + rpz * rpz)\n    bmag = np.sqrt(b2)\n    d[\"cos_theta_b\"] = np.where(\n        has_b, (rpx * bx + rpy * by + rpz * bz) / np.maximum(rmag * bmag, 1e-9), 0.0)\n\n    # ---- W-daughter balance / shape --------------------------------------\n    pt_sorted = np.sort(pt_arr, axis=1)\n    d[\"pt_asym_bc\"] = (pt_sorted[:, 2] - pt_sorted[:, 0]) / np.maximum(sum_pt, 1e-9)\n    nb_pt = np.where(has_b[:, None], np.where(btmat > 0, np.nan, pt_arr), pt_arr)\n    with np.errstate(invalid=\"ignore\"):\n        nb_max = np.nanmax(nb_pt, axis=1)\n        nb_min = np.nanmin(nb_pt, axis=1)\n    d[\"pt_asym_w\"] = (nb_max - nb_min) / np.maximum(nb_max + nb_min, 1e-9)\n    d[\"m_over_pt_a\"] = m[ia] / np.maximum(pt_a, 1e-9)\n    d[\"m_over_pt_b\"] = m[ib] / np.maximum(pt_b, 1e-9)\n    d[\"m_over_pt_c\"] = m[ic] / np.maximum(pt_c, 1e-9)\n\n    # ---- event context ---------------------------------------------------\n    d[\"n_genjet\"] = np.full(len(ia), float(n_all))\n    d[\"ht_event\"] = np.full(len(ia), ht)\n    d[\"idx_i\"] = ia.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_j\"] = ib.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_k\"] = ic.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_sum\"] = (ia + ib + within the event (0 = best).\"\"\"\n    n = len(x)\n    if n == 1:\n        return np.zeros(1)\n    order = np.argsort(x if ascending else -x, kind=\"mergesort\")\n    r = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)\n    r[order] = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)\n    return r / (n - 1)\n\n\ndef _event_context_features(pt, eta, phi, m, bt, px, py, pz, e, combos,\n                            base, n, ht):\n    \"\"\"Features that compare a triplet with the rest of its own event.\n\n    These give the classifier the combinatorial context it needs: a triplet\n    with |m-m_top| = 15 GeV is compelling if every alternative in the event is\n    far off, and unremarkable if twenty alternatives are closer.\n    \"\"\"\n    d = {}\n    ncomb = len(combos)\n    idx_ar = np.arange(ncomb)\n\n --------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef greedy_disjoint_select(df: pd.DataFrame, score_col: str = \"score_xgb\",\n                           max_per_event: int = 2, threshold: float = -np.inf,\n                           second_threshold: float | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Per event: take the highest-scoring triplet, then the highest-scoring\n    remaining triplet sharing no jet index with anything already taken.\"\"\"\n    if second_threshold is None:\n        second_threshold = threshold\n    out = []\n    cols = [\"event_id\", \"i\", Because the target metric is pure triplet-level recall, the expected number\n    of recovered truth triplets for a chosen set S is sum_{t in S} P(t truthy).\n    Maximising the summed calibrated  can be forced into a bad second pick by a slightly-higher-scoring fake.\n    \"\"\"\n    if second_threshold is None:\n        second_threshold = threshold\n    out = []\n    for ev, g in tp = int(((y == 1) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fp = int(((y == 0) & (p == 1)).sum())\n    fn = int(((y == 1) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    tn = int(((y == 0) & (p == 0)).sum())\n    prec = tp / (tp + fp) if tp + fp else 0.0\n    rec = tp / (tp + fn) if tp + fn else 0.0\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if prec + rec else 0.0\n    s = np.clip(np.asarray(score, dtype=np.float64), 1e-9, 1 - 1e-9)\n bt, px, py, pz, e,\n                      ia, ib, ic, ht, n_all, n_btag_ev):\n    d = {}\n    pt_a, pt_b, pt_c = pt[ia], pt[ib], pt[ic]\n    eta_a, eta_b, eta_c = eta[ia], eta[ib], eta[ic]\n    phi_a, PY, PZ, E = px[u] + px[v], py[u] + py[v], pz[u] + pz[v], e[u] + e[v]\n        return _mass(PX, PY, PZ, E), np.hypot(PX, PY)\n\n    m_ab, ptp_ab = pair(ia, ib)\n    m_ac, ptp_ac = pair(ia, ic)\n    m_bc, ptp_bc = pair(ib, ic)\n\n    PX = px[ia] + px[ib] + px[ic]\n    PY = py[ia] + py[ib] + py[ic]\n    PZ = pz[ia] + pz[ib] + pz[ic]\n    E = e[ia] + e[ib] + e[ic]\n    m123 = _mass(PX, PY, PZ, E)\n    pt123 = np.hypot(PX, PY)\n    p123 = np.sqrt(PX * PX + PY * PY + PZ * PZ)\n    eta123 = np.arcsinh(np.divide(PZ, np.maximum(pt123, 1e-9)))\n    phi123 = np.arctan2(PY, PX)\n    denom = schema columns ---------------------------------------\n    d[\"dr_ab\"], d[\"dr_ac\"], d[\"dr_bc\"] = dr_ab, dr_ac, dr_bc\n    d[\"mij_over_m123_ab\"] = m_ab / denom\n    d[\"mij_over_m123_ac\"] = m_ac / denom\n    d[\"mij_over_m123_bc\"] = m_bc / denom\n\n    # ---- single-jet kinematics ------------------------------------------\n    d[\"pt_a\"], d[\"pt_b\"], d[\"pt_c\"] = pt_a, pt_b, pt_c\n    d[\"abseta_a\"], d[\"abseta_b\"], d[\"abseta_c\"] = np.abs(eta_a), np.abs(eta_b), np.abs(eta_c)\n    d[\"m_a\"], d[\"m_b\"], d[\"m_c\"] = m[ia], m[ib], m[ic]\n    d[\"btag_a\"], d[\"btag_b\"], d[\"m_bc\"] = m_ab, m_ac, m_bc\n    pair_m = np.stack([m_ab, m_ac, m_bc], axis=1)\n    pair_dr = np.stack([dr_ab, dr_ac, dr_bc], axis=1)\n    pair_pt = np.stack([ptp_ab, ptp_ac, ptp_bc], axis=1)\n    d[\"m_pair_min\"] = pair_m.min(axis=1)\n    d[\"m_pair_max\"] = pair_m.max(axis=1)\n    d[\"m_pair_med\"] = np.median(pair_m, axis=1)\n\n    # W candidate = pair with mass closest to m_W\n    w_idx = np.argmin(np.abs(pair_m - M_W), axis=1)\n    rowsel = np.arange(len(ia))\n    m_w = pair_m[rowsel, w_idx]\n    d[\"m_wcand\"] = m_w\n    d[\"dm_wcand\"] = m_w - M_W\n    d[\"absdm_wcand\"] = non-b jets when exactly 1 b-tag\n    btmat = np.stack([bt_a, bt_b, bt_c], axis=1)\n    # index of the (first) b-tagged jet, -1 if none\n    has_b = btmat.max(axis=1) > 0\n    b_pos = np.where(has_b, np.where(has_b, opp[rowsel, np.maximum(b_pos, 0)], m_w)\n    d[\"m_w_nonb\"] = m_wb\n    d[\"absdm_w_nonb\"] = np.abs(m_wb - M_W)\n    d[\"dr_w_nonb\"] = np.where(has_b, opp_dr[rowsel, np.maximum(b_pos, 0)], d[\"dr_wcand\"])\n    pt_arr = np.stack([pt_a, pt_b, pt_c], axis=1)\n    d[\"pt_bjet\"] = np.where(has_b, pt_arr[rowsel, np.maximum(b_pos, 0)], 0.0)\n\n    # ---- triplet-level ---------------------------------------------------\n    d[\"m123\"] = m123\n    d[\"pt123\"] = pt123\n    d[\"abseta123\"] = np.abs(eta123)\n    d[\"absdm_top\"] = np.abs(m123 - M_TOP)\n    d[\"m123_over_ht\"] = m123 / max(ht, 1e-9)\n    d[\"pt123_over_ht\"] = pt123 / max(ht, 1e-9)\n    sum_pt = pt_a + pt_b + pt_c\n    d[\"sumpt_over_ht\"] = sum_pt / max(ht, 1e-9)\n    d[\"sumpt\"] = sum_pt\n    d[\"pt_frac_a\"] = pt_a / sum_pt\n    d[\"pt_frac_b\"] = pt_b / sum_pt\n    d[\"pt_frac_c\"] = pt_c / sum_pt\n    d[\"pt123_over_m123\"] = pt123 / denom\n    d[\"boost\"] = p123 / denom\n\n    d[\"dr_sum\"] = dr_ab + dr_ac + dr_bc\n    d[\"dr_min\"] = np.minimum(np.minimum(dr_ab, dr_ac), dr_bc)\n    d[\"dr_max\"] = np.maximum(np.maximum(dr_ab, dr_ac), dr_bc)\n    d[\"deta_max\"] = np.maximum.reduce(\n        [np.abs(eta_a - eta_b), np.abs(eta_a - eta_c), np.abs(eta_b - eta_c)]\n    )\n    # dR of each jet to the triplet axis\n    d[\"dr_a_123\"] = delta_r(eta_a, phi_a, eta123, phi123)\n    d[\"dr_b_123\"] = delta_r(eta_b, phi_b, eta123, phi123)\n    d[\"dr_c_123\"] = delta_r(eta_c, phi_c, eta123, phi123)\n\n    # ---- W-mass-constrained top mass -------------------------------------\n    # Rescaling the triplet mass by M_W / m(non-b pair) cancels most of the\n    # jet-energy-scale / out-of-cone smearing and is a far sharper top\n    # discriminant than the raw m123.\n    m123_wcorr = m123 * M_W / np.maximum(m_wb, 1e-6)\n    d[\"m123_wcorr\"] = m123_wcorr\n    d[\"absdm_top_wcorr\"] = np.abs(m123_wcorr - M_TOP)\n    d[\"m123_minus_mw\"] = m123 - m_wb\n    chi2 = ((m123 - M_TOP) / 20.0) ** 2 + ((m_wb - M_W) / 12.0) ** 2\n    d[\"chi2_topw\"] = chi2\n    d[\"log_chi2_topw\"] = np.log1p(chi2)\n\n    # ---- decay angles ----------------------------------------------------\n    # cos(theta*) of the b jet in the triplet rest frame w.r.t. the triplet\n    # flight direction: flat for a real top decay, peaked for random combos.\n    bx, by, bz = PX / np.maximum(E, 1e-9), PY / np.maximum(E, 1e-9), PZ / np.maximum(E, 1e-9)\n    b2 = bx * bx + by * by + bz * bz\n    gamma = 1.0 / np.sqrt(np.maximum(1.0 - b2, e[ib], e[ic]], axis=1)\n    sel_b = np.maximum(b_pos, 0)\n    jpx, jpy, jpz, je = (px3[rowsel, sel_b], py3[rowsel, sel_b],\n                         pz3[rowsel, sel_b], e3[rowsel, sel_b])\n    bp = bx * jpx + by * jpy + bz * jpz\n    fac = (gamma - 1.0) * np.divide(bp, np.maximum(b2, 1e-12)) - gamma * je\n    rpx, rpy, rpz = jpx + fac * bx, jpy + fac * by, jpz + fac * bz\n    rmag = np.sqrt(rpx * rpx + rpy * rpy + rpz * rpz)\n    bmag = np.sqrt(b2)\n    d[\"cos_theta_b\"] = np.where(\n        has_b, (rpx * bx + rpy * by + rpz * bz) / np.maximum(rmag * bmag, 1e-9), 0.0)\n\n    # ---- W-daughter d[\"m_over_pt_c\"] = m[ic] / np.maximum(pt_c, 1e-9)\n\n    # ---- event context ---------------------------------------------------\n    d[\"n_genjet\"] = np.full(len(ia), float(n_all))\n    d[\"ht_event\"] = np.full(len(ia), ht)\n    d[\"idx_i\"] = ia.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_j\"] = ib.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_k\"] = ic.astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"idx_sum\"] = (ia + ib + within the event (0 = best).\"\"\"\n    n = len(x)\n    if n == 1:\n        return np.zeros(1)\n    order = np.argsort(x if ascending else -x, kind=\"mergesort\")\n    r = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)\n    r[order] = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)\n    return r / (n - 1)\n\n\ndef _event_context_features(pt, eta, phi, m, bt, px, py, pz, e, combos,\n                            base, n, ht):\n    \"\"\"Features that compare a triplet with the rest of its own event.\n\n    These give the classifier the combinatorial context it needs: a triplet\n    with |m-m_top| = 15 GeV is compelling if every alternative in the event is\n    far off, and unremarkable if twenty alternatives are closer.\n    \"\"\"\n    d = {}\n    ncomb = len(combos)\n    idx_ar = np.arange(ncomb)\n\n    # ---- rest-of-event (jets not in the triplet) -------------------------\n    inmask = np.zeros((ncomb, n), dtype=bool)\n    inmask[idx_ar[:, None], combos] = True\n    out = ~inmask\n    d[\"n_jet_outside\"] = out.sum(axis=1).astype(np.float64)\n    d[\"ht_outside\"] = out @ pt\n    d[\"n_btag_outside\"] = out @ bt\n    oPX, oPY, oPZ, oE = out @ px, out @ py, out @ pz, out @ e\n    d[\"m_outside\"] = _mass(oPX, oPY, oPZ, oE)\n    d[\"pt_outside\"] = np.hypot(oPX, oPY)\n\n    # ---- comparison against alternative triplets in the same event -------\n    absdm = base[\"absdm_top\"]\n    absdw = base[\"absdm_w_nonb\"]\n    d[\"absdm_top_rank\"] = _rank_pct(absdm)\n    d[\"absdm_w_rank\"] = _rank_pct(absdw)\n    d[\"pt123_rank\"] = _rank_pct(base[\"pt123\"], ascending=False)\n    d[\"sumpt_rank\"] = _rank_pct(base[\"sumpt\"], ascending=False)\n    d[\"drsum_rank\"] = _rank_pct(base[\"dr_sum\"])\n    ev_min = absdm.min()\n    d[\"absdm_top_minus_evmin\"] = absdm - ev_min\n    d[\"absdm_w_minus_evmin\"] = absdw - absdw.min()\n    d[\"n_alt_better_mass\"] = np.array(\n        [(absdm < a).sum() for a in absdm], dtype=np.float64)\n    d[\"n_triplet_1btag\"] = np.full(ncomb, float((base[\"n_btag\"] == 1).sum()))\n\n    chi2 = base[\"chi2_topw\"]\n    absdmw = base[\"absdm_top_wcorr\"]\n    d[\"chi2_rank\"] = _rank_pct(chi2)\n    d[\"chi2_minus_evmin\"] = chi2 - chi2.min()\n    d[\"absdm_wcorr_rank\"] = _rank_pct(absdmw)\n    d[\"absdm_wcorr_minus_evmin\"] = absdmw - absdmw.min()\n    d[\"n_alt_better_chi2\"] = np.array([(chi2
